A war between [Israel](9282173.md) and [Hezbollah](13919.md) took place in [Lebanon](17771.md) during 2024 amid the [Middle Eastern crisis](75895273.md). The war began in September 2024[^cite_note-88][^cite_note-89][^cite_note-90][^cite_note-91] following nearly 12 months of [conflict](75005425.md) between Israel and Hezbollah, when the former initiated major attacks in Lebanon including [an attack on pagers and electronic devices](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Lebanon_electronic_device_attacks),[^cite_note-92][^cite_note-AP-News-2024b22-93][^cite_note-reuters20240930-94] the [assassination of Hezbollah leader Hassan Nasrallah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Hassan_Nasrallah),[^cite_note-apnews20240928-95][^cite_note-96] and [an aerial bombing campaign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/September_2024_Israeli_attacks_against_Lebanon) throughout the country. The war escalated on 1 October, when Israel crossed the [Blue Line](987817.md) in its sixth [invasion of Lebanon](6020082.md) since 1978.[^cite_note-97] On 26 November, Israel and Lebanon [signed a ceasefire agreement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Israel%E2%80%93Lebanon_ceasefire_agreement), mediated by France and the United States.[^cite_note-98][^cite_note-99] The ceasefire went into effect on 27 November, though some attacks continued as part of the broader conflict.

Hostilities between Hezbollah and Israel erupted shortly after [Hamas' October 7 attack](74998317.md) on Israel, when Hezbollah joined the conflict in support of [Hamas](13913.md), launching rockets into northern Israel and the [Israeli-occupied](2781576.md) [Golan Heights](7515865.md). Cross borders attacks resulted in a large number of displaced people on both sides of the border. Prior to the war, Israel had requested that Hezbollah implement [United Nations Security Council Resolution 1701](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1701) (UNSCR 1701) and withdraw its forces north of the [Litani River](1920925.md).[^cite_note-UNSCcondemn-100][^cite_note-\:0-101] Hezbollah vowed to keep fighting until Israel halted its operations in Gaza. Diplomatic efforts from the United States and France proved ineffective in resolving the conflict by 18 September.&#x20;

Afterwards, Israel commenced attacks throughout Lebanon throughout the rest of the month. The attacks saw heavy casualties, killing over 800 Lebanese people in one week in late September.[^cite_note-foreignaffairs20240923-102] Israel stated that it had been operating to remove the threat that Hezbollah's military capabilities was posing.[^cite_note-GD0110-103][^cite_note-apnews20241001-104] At the start of the invasion in October, the [Lebanese Armed Forces](17779.md) (LAF) withdrew from parts of the [Blue Line](987817.md).[^cite_note-reuters20240929-105] On 27 November, the ceasefire agreement came into effect. Israel has reported 56 of its soldiers and 2,762 Hezbollah militants killed in the invasion,[^cite_note-timesofisrael20241225-73] while the Lebanese government has reported Israel killing 2,720 people in Lebanon, mostly civilians.[^cite_note-timep20241114-106][^cite_note-barrons20241106-65]

Under the ceasefire agreement, Israeli forces were to withdraw from Lebanon by 26 January 2025, but Israel refused to do so, leading to a new deadline of Israeli withdrawal by 18 February 2025. Israel did not fully withdraw by the new deadline, as it withdrew troops from Lebanese villages but kept Israeli forces maintaining five military outposts on highlands in Southern Lebanon.

## Background

Main articles: [Gaza war](74998519.md) and [Israel–Hezbollah conflict (2023–2024)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israel%E2%80%93Hezbollah_conflict_\(2023%E2%80%932024\))

The last time Israel invaded southern Lebanon and engaged in ground combat with Hezbollah was during the month-long [2006 Lebanon War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_Lebanon_War).[^cite_note-apnews20240930-107]

According to security expert Sobelman, Israel had spent two decades preparing for the next war with Hezbollah.[^cite_note-cnn20241012-108]

Shortly after the onset of the [Gaza war](74998519.md) in October 2023, Hezbollah joined the conflict, citing solidarity with Palestinians,[^cite_note-aj-109] which quickly escalated into regular cross-border military exchanges impacting areas in or around [northern Israel](324281.md), [southern Lebanon](4205968.md) and the Israeli-occupied [Golan Heights](7515865.md) of [Syria](7515849.md).[^cite_note-110] Hezbollah said it aimed to pressure Israel by forcing it to fight on two fronts.[^cite_note-foreignaffairs20240923-102] Hezbollah has offered an immediate ceasefire should [a ceasefire also happen in Gaza](75313349.md).[^cite_note-Hezbollah_warns-111][^cite_note-112] From 8 October 2023 to 20 September 2024, Hezbollah has launched 1,900 cross border attacks, and Israel has launched another 8,300.[^cite_note-aljazeera20240925-113] The fighting killed 564 in Lebanon (including 133 civilians),[^cite_note-leb-displace-114] and 52 in Israel (including 27 civilians), displaced entire communities in Israel and Lebanon,[^cite_note-leb-displace-114] with significant damage to civilian infrastructure.[^cite_note-115]

Israel requested that Hezbollah implement [United Nations Security Council Resolution 1701](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1701) (UNSCR 1701) and withdraw its forces north of the [Litani River](1920925.md).[^cite_note-UNSCcondemn-100][^cite_note-\:0-101] Hezbollah has stated it will continue attacking Israel until Israel halts [its operations in Gaza](74998519.md).[^cite_note-aljazeera20240916-116] Both Israel and Hezbollah have outstanding obligations under UNSCR 1701.[^cite_note-117][^cite_note-UNSCcondemn-100][^cite_note-securitycouncilreport20230713-118] Hezbollah has established strong military presence in southern Lebanon, storing rockets in civilian sites, building tunnels into Israel, and obstructing [United Nations Interim Force in Lebanon](787766.md)'s (UNIFIL) access.[^cite_note-Vohra2-119][^cite_note-nytimes20241001-120] Israel continues to occupy Ghajar and an adjacent area,[^cite_note-securitycouncilreport20230713-118][^cite_note-121] and has repeatedly breached Lebanese airspace, waters, and borders.[^cite_note-122][^cite_note-123][^cite_note-124] UNIFIL reports that Israel entered Lebanese airspace on more than 22,000 occasions between 2007 and 2021.[^cite_note-125][^cite_note-126] Diplomatic efforts, led by US envoy [Amos Hochstein](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amos_Hochstein) and [France](5843419.md), were not effective in resolving the conflict by 18 September 2024.[^cite_note-127][^cite_note-128]

## Start and prelude to invasion

### September 2024 escalation

Main articles: [2024 Lebanon electronic device attacks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Lebanon_electronic_device_attacks), [20 September 2024 Beirut attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20_September_2024_Beirut_attack), [September 2024 Israeli attacks against Lebanon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/September_2024_Israeli_attacks_against_Lebanon), and [2024 Hezbollah headquarters strike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Hezbollah_headquarters_strike)

On 15 September, leaflets bearing an IDF logo were observed in southern Lebanon. They contained the text, "Hezbollah is firing from your area", urging residents to relocate north and to refrain from returning "until the war is over". The IDF said that they were distributed without senior authorization and that the incident was being investigated.[^cite_note-129]

On 17 and 18 September, thousands of Hezbollah's handheld [pagers and walkie-talkies exploded](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Lebanon_electronic_device_attacks) in a coordinated series of attacks.[^cite_note-CNN-3-130] The explosions killed 42 people and injured at least 3,500, including civilians.[^cite_note-131][^cite_note-NWS_1-132][^cite_note-ABCN_1-133] Reuters reported that, according to an unnamed Hezbollah official, 1,500 Hezbollah fighters were taken out of action due to injuries, with many blinded or having lost their hands.[^cite_note-134] Despite Israel denying involvement with the attack,[^cite_note-denial-135] unnamed Israeli sources told Reuters and other media that it was orchestrated by Israel's intelligence service ([Mossad](27619007.md)) and military.[^cite_note-136][^cite_note-nyt-fbs-trojan-24-137][^cite_note-CNN-3-130] In response, Hezbollah, who described the attack as a possible [declaration of war](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Declaration_of_war) by Israel, launched a rocket attack on northern Israel a few days later.[^cite_note-Reuters-2-138][^cite_note-139]

On 20 September, tensions further rose after [Ibrahim Aqil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ibrahim_Aqil) [was killed in an Israeli strike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20_September_2024_Beirut_attack) in [Beirut](37428.md), along with other senior commanders from the unit.[^cite_note-140][^cite_note-141]

People in southern Lebanon and Beirut received mobile phone calls and messages that told them to relocate.[^cite_note-142] IDF spokesperson Daniel Hagari said in a video address, "We advise civilians from Lebanese villages located in and next to buildings and areas used by Hezbollah for military purposes, such as those used to store weapons, to immediately evacuate for their own safety".[^cite_note-143]

The IDF [began a series of airstrikes on 23 September](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/September_2024_Israeli_attacks_against_Lebanon), killing over 800 and injuring more than 5,000 in the first week.[^cite_note-NYTimesInfo-144][^cite_note-145]

On 27 September 2024, [Hassan Nasrallah](313436.md), the [secretary-general of Hezbollah](9364927.md), and several other senior Hezbollah leaders, including [Ali Karaki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ali_Karaki), commander of Hezbollah's southern front, were killed in an [Israeli airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Hezbollah_headquarters_strike) in [Beirut](37428.md).[^cite_note-146][^cite_note-apnews20240928-95] The strike took place while Hezbollah leaders were meeting at a [headquarters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hezbollah_Headquarters) located underground beneath residential buildings in [Haret Hreik](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haret_Hreik) in the [Dahieh](6082788.md) suburb to the south of Beirut.[^cite_note-apnews20240928-95][^cite_note-Haaretz-147]

### Previous Israeli oprtations

On 1 October, the IDF revealed that its special forces had carried out more than 70 small covert operations into southern Lebanon since November 2023, but had not clashed with Hezbollah fighters during these raids.[^cite_note-148] The IDF said it had uncovered Hezbollah tunnels, weaponry, and invasion plans in villages near the border, including [Ayta ash-Shaab](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayta_ash-Shaab), [Mais al-Jabal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mais_al-Jabal) and [Kfar Kila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kfar_Kila,_Marjayoun).[^cite_note-jpost_1oct-149] IDF Spokesman [Daniel Hagari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Hagari) said they found Hezbollah plans to launch an attack similar to Hamas' [7 October attacks](74998317.md), which sparked the conflict.[^cite_note-reuters20240930-94] Hagari also mentioned that evidence from these operations, including videos and maps, would be presented to the international community. Hezbollah had not commented on his claims by the end of September.[^cite_note-reuters20240930-94] Hagari alleged that the group was preparing for an attack similar to the [7 October Hamas-led attack on Israel](74998317.md).[^cite_note-apnews20241001-104][^cite_note-reuters20240930-94]

### Preparations for invasion

On 30 September, Israel informed the [United States](3434750.md) that it intended to carry out a ground maneuver in [Lebanon](17771.md) aimed at clearing [Hezbollah](13919.md)'s militant infrastructure along the border. That evening, the [Lebanese Armed Forces](17779.md) (LAF) and [United Nations Interim Force in Lebanon](787766.md) (UNIFIL) withdrew from the [Israeli-Lebanese border](987817.md) to the north to a distance of 5 kilometres (3.1 mi; 2.7 nmi) from the border, and the [Israel Defense Forces](39237.md) (IDF) declared that the communities of [Metula](2599433.md), [Misgav Am](12960865.md) and [Kfar Giladi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kfar_Giladi) are a closed military area.[^cite_note-150] Since then the LAF has reported an incident of fire exchange with the IDF.[^cite_note-lebarmy2-13] IDF troops were amassing on the border in [Southern Lebanon](4205968.md), with Israel stating that they were preparing for a limited ground operation,[^cite_note-151][^cite_note-152][^cite_note-GD0110-103][^cite_note-153] with no intentions for long-term occupation.[^cite_note-154]

## Invasion and timeline

For a chronological guide, see [Timeline of the Israel–Hezbollah conflict (17 September – 26 November 2024)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Timeline_of_the_Israel%E2%80%93Hezbollah_conflict_\(17_September_%E2%80%93_26_November_2024\)) and [Timeline of the Israel–Hezbollah conflict (27 November 2024 – present)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Timeline_of_the_Israel%E2%80%93Hezbollah_conflict_\(27_November_2024_%E2%80%93_present\)).

### 1 October

Main articles: [Battle of Odaisseh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Odaisseh), [2024 Ayta al-Shaab clashes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Ayta_al-Shaab_clashes), and [2024 Kafr Kila clashes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Kafr_Kila_clashes)



Map at the start of the invasion

On 1 October, IDF troops crossed the border into Lebanon in a series of small scale raids intended to precede a broader ground incursion.[^cite_note-155][^cite_note-nbcnews20240930-156][^cite_note-157] Witnesses have said that they have heard sounds of tanks in Southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-158][^cite_note-apnews20240930-107] There were also reports of heavy artillery striking the border towns of Southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-159] On 1 October, the IDF confirmed the existence of their ground operation in a statement on [Telegram](41501305.md), specifying that they intended to strike Hezbollah infrastructure. Shortly after the release of this statement, IDF Spokesperson [Daniel Hagari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Hagari) also confirmed the operation.[^cite_note-160] Prior to the release of these statements, the IDF had made [no announcements](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Policy_of_deliberate_ambiguity) about the operation. The IDF ordered residents of 25 villages in southern Lebanon to immediately relocate to north of the [Awali River](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Awali_\(river\)).[^cite_note-161]

IDF Arabic-language spokesperson [Avichay Adraee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avichay_Adraee) said that heavy fighting was taking place in southern Lebanon with Hezbollah. He warned residents to not move in vehicles from north of [Litani River](1920925.md) to south of the river. The IDF also said that projectiles were launched to [Avivim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avivim) and Metula.[^cite_note-162] However, IDF spokesperson [Daniel Hagari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Hagari) said they were not yet fighting Hezbollah face-to-face.[^cite_note-163] Another Israeli military official said that they had not yet clashed with Hezbollah on the ground, and that IDF troops had so far been operating in villages only hundreds of meters across the border. Hezbollah denied that Israeli troops had entered Lebanon.[^cite_note-164]

The IDF said that in coordination with the [Intelligence Division](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_Intelligence_Directorate_\(Israel\)), IDF forces led by the [Northern Command](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northern_Command_\(Israel\)) and [special forces](77766.md) had captured Hezbollah infrastructure in the towns of [Kafr Kila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafr_Kila,_Lebanon), [Ayta ash Shab](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayta_ash_Shab), [Meiss Ej Jabal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meiss_Ej_Jabal) amongst other sites and destroyed the facilities after they were abandoned after the retreat of Hezbollah.[^cite_note-MID-34] Hezbollah also claimed that it had targeted IDF soldiers in towns opposite of [Odaisseh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Odaisseh) and [Kafr Kila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafr_Kila,_Lebanon).[^cite_note-165]

The IAF struck 100 Hezbollah targets in Lebanon while soldiers of the Northern Command destroyed several military sites and weapons.[^cite_note-166]

An Israeli strike on a house in Al-Dawoudiya killed at least ten people and injured five others.[^cite_note-167]

#### Retaliation by Hezbollah

Hezbollah launched ten rockets from southern Lebanon to Israel[^cite_note-168] and a drone towards central Israel.[^cite_note-169] Hezbollah said that it targeted IDF sites, Israeli communities and soldiers with 12 separate strikes.[^cite_note-170] Three rockets were launched from Lebanon towards Upper Galilee.[^cite_note-171] Hezbollah said that it targeted Israeli soldiers in [Metula](2599433.md) with rockets and artillery.[^cite_note-172]

"A number of" rockets were launched from Lebanon to Israel, moderately injuring two people.[^cite_note-173][^cite_note-174] Hezbollah said that it targeted [Unit 8200](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unit_8200) headquarters in [Tel Aviv](31453.md) with [Fadi-4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fadi-4?action=edit\&redlink=1) rockets and Mossad headquarters in the suburbs of Tel Aviv.[^cite_note-intunits-35] Approximately 30 rockets were launched from Lebanon towards northern Israel.[^cite_note-175] Hezbollah said that its missiles targeted [Sde Dov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sde_Dov), an airport and airbase in the outskirts of Tel Aviv that was closed and demolished more than four years before the attack.[^cite_note-176][^cite_note-177]

#### Israeli strikes elsewhere

The IDF called for evacuations of at least 500 meters from particular buildings with Hezbollah facilities in parts of southern [Beirut](37428.md), the capital of Lebanon, as they were preparing to bomb them.[^cite_note-nbcnews20240930-156] The IDF conducted at least two airstrikes in [Dahieh](6082788.md).[^cite_note-178] The IDF said that Muhammad Jaafar Qasir, a Hezbollah commander responsible for transferring Iranian weapons to Hezbollah in Lebanon, was killed in an airstrike in Beirut.[^cite_note-179] The IDF said that it killed the commander of Hezbollah's Imam Hussein division in an airstrike in Beirut.[^cite_note-ImamHusseinDivision-38]

An Israeli strike on the house of Munir al-Maqdah, a brigadier general of [Al-Aqsa Martyrs' Brigades](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Aqsa_Martyrs'_Brigades) in Lebanon in [Ein al-Hilweh refugee camp](5901773.md) killed at least five people.[^cite_note-AQMB-180]

[Syrian military](27479.md) sources said that Israel struck two Syrian anti-aircraft radar stations west of [Sweida](2590570.md) and a Syrian anti-aircraft radar station in [Daraa Governorate](5317030.md).[^cite_note-181]

#### Iranian strikes on Israel

Iran's [Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps](1285472.md) (IRGC) [launched at least 181 missiles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2024_Iranian_strikes_against_Israel) toward Israel, with at least one building being hit in Tel Aviv.[^cite_note-182] The attack was in response to the assassination of [Abbas Nilforoushan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abbas_Nilforoushan), [Hassan Nasrallah](313436.md) and [Ismail Haniyeh](41207463.md). At least two Israelis were injured in Tel Aviv and a Palestinian was killed by shrapnel in [Nu'eima](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nu'eima?action=edit\&redlink=1), West Bank.[^cite_note-183][^cite_note-184][^cite_note-185]

### 2 October



Command center of the [98th Paratroopers Division](12871166.md) during the Israeli invasion of southern Lebanon

On 2 October, Israeli forces were [ambushed by Hezbollah fighters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Odaisseh) in [Odaisseh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Odaisseh) and forced to retreat while attempting to dismantle militant infrastructure. Six soldiers from the [Egoz Unit](4311894.md) were killed and several others were injured, including five seriously. The IDF said that another 20 Hezbollah militants were killed during the clash.[^cite_note-186][^cite_note-timesofisrael20241002-187] Two soldiers of the [Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade) were killed and another was injured in combat in southern Lebanon. Hezbollah said it destroyed three [Merkava](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Merkava) tanks with guided missiles as they approached Maroun al-Ras.[^cite_note-188] A medic from the brigade was injured in a separate incident.[^cite_note-timesofisrael20241002-187] The IDF also announced that an officer of the [Paratroopers Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paratroopers_Brigade)'s 202nd Battalion was killed in combat.[^cite_note-189]

An Israeli air strike destroyed three houses in the [Bekaa Valley](2162385.md) killing 11 people.[^cite_note-190]

More forces from the [IDF 36th Division](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/36th_Division_\(Israel\)) entered southern Lebanon including the [Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade), the [188th Armored Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/188th_Armored_Brigade_\(Israel\)), the [Etzioni Reserve Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Etzioni_Brigade) and the [282nd Artillery Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/282nd_Artillery_Brigade?action=edit\&redlink=1).[^cite_note-36bg-191][^cite_note-192]

Hezbollah fired around 100 rockets at northern Israel.[^cite_note-193] Hezbollah said that it targeted areas north of [Haifa](55606.md) using missiles.[^cite_note-194] It also targeted [Shtula](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shtula) and Israeli [infantry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Infantry) in [Misgav Am](12960865.md).[^cite_note-195]

#### Beirut suburbs strike

Main article: [2024 Maroun al-Ras clashes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Maroun_al-Ras_clashes)

Israeli aircraft struck the southern suburbs of Beirut.[^cite_note-196] Israeli soldiers from the 98th division's two Brigades managed to capture a Hezbollah installation in southern Lebanon capturing a large cache of weaponry, ammunition amongst other things as well as claiming to have captured/destroyed 150 Hezbollah targets in Southern Lebanon in coordination with IAF.[^cite_note-197] After the airstrike, Beirut residents reported a [sulphur](27127.md)-like smell. The [National News Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_News_Agency_\(Lebanon\)) reported that Israel had used [phosphorus bombs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_phosphorus_munition) in the heavily populated Bashoura district. According to Protocol III of the [Convention on Certain Conventional Weapons](3111840.md), the use of phosphorus bombs in civilian areas is prohibited.[^cite_note-198]

Hezbollah engaged Israeli soldiers entering [Maroun al-Ras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maroun_al-Ras) from its eastern side claiming to have inflicted several casualties amongst the Israelis.[^cite_note-199] The LAF said that an IDF force crossed the [Blue Line](987817.md) in the areas of Khirbet Yaroun and Odaisseh and claimed they retreated after an incursion of about 400m.[^cite_note-200] The IDF announced that the IAF struck 150 Hezbollah sites since the ground offensive began.[^cite_note-201] A LAF soldier was injured in an IDF drone strike while one of its units was working to open a road at the entrance of [Kawkaba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kawkaba,_Lebanon).[^cite_note-202]

Hezbollah claimed that it killed or injured Israeli soldiers trying to circumvent [Yaroun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yaroun) by detonating an explosive device.[^cite_note-203]

Hezbollah fired 40 rockets at Safed and several drones at the Upper Galilee.[^cite_note-204]

#### Deaths and injuries

The IDF announced the death of nine soldiers during combat in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-205][^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58] It also said that since the start of the invasion, 50 Hezbollah militants have been killed, with thirty of them being killed in a single battle with the [Egoz unit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Egoz_unit). Moreover, thirty Israeli soldiers were wounded, including seven seriously which were evacuated by [Unit 669](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unit_669).[^cite_note-Hezcasualties-32]

Hezbollah claimed to have destroyed three Israeli [Merkava](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Merkava) tanks with guided missiles as they approached Maroun al-Ras.[^cite_note-Rabinovitch-206][^cite_note-207]

#### Damascus drone strike

[Syrian](7515849.md) state media reported that at least three civilians were killed and three injured in an Israeli drone strike in [Damascus](8914.md).[^cite_note-208] The strike killed the brother of the commander of Hezbollah's Unit 4400, who was also killed the day prior.[^cite_note-unit4400-40]

Hezbollah claimed to have killed or injured all members of an Israeli infantry unit sheltering in a home outside [Kafr Kila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafr_Kila,_Lebanon) by detonating an explosive device in the house and targeting it using bullets and rocket-propelled grenades.[^cite_note-209] Hezbollah said that it targeted Israeli soldiers in [Ya'ara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ya'ara) with rockets.[^cite_note-210]

#### Beirut health facility strike

Main article: [2024 Beirut medical center airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Beirut_medical_center_airstrike)

An Israeli airstrike on a Hezbollah-affiliated health facility a kilometer away from the [Lebanese Parliament](1618242.md) in central Beirut killed nine people, including seven paramedics, and injured 14 other people.[^cite_note-211][^cite_note-212]

### 3 October

#### Attack on the Lebanese army and response

The LAF stated that the IDF targeted an army post in the [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md) area, and the LAF — for the first time in the invasion — fired back at the IDF.[^cite_note-lebarmy2-13] One LAF soldier was killed.[^cite_note-lebarmy2-13] Another LAF soldier was killed and four [Lebanese Red Cross](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lebanese_Red_Cross) medics and another LAF soldier were injured in an Israeli strike in [Taybeh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taybeh_\(Marjaayoun\)) while they were working on a rescue and evacuation mission.[^cite_note-213][^cite_note-214]

The LAF said that one of its soldiers was killed in an Israeli strike on a military outpost in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md). It also said that its soldiers responded to the sources of fire.[^cite_note-215]

#### Hezbollah strikes

Two drones targeted [Bat Yam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bat_Yam) in central Israel, one of which was shot down and the other hit an open area.[^cite_note-216]

Hezbollah said that it launched surface-to-air missiles targeting an IDF helicopter flying above [Beit Hillel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beit_Hillel) claiming that it forced it to retreat.[^cite_note-217] Approximately 25 rockets and two drones were launched from Lebanon towards Israel.[^cite_note-218]

Hezbollah launched more than a dozen of strikes on Israeli targets.[^cite_note-marsi-varshalomidze-20241003-219] Hezbollah claimed to have killed or wounded a group of Israeli soldiers in the vicinity of [Maroun al-Ras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maroun_al-Ras) by detonating two explosive devices.[^cite_note-marsi-varshalomidze-20241003-219][^cite_note-220]

Hezbollah fired ten rockets at the Lower Galilee, causing no casualties.[^cite_note-221]

Hezbollah claimed that it detonated a [Sejil bomb](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sejil_bomb?action=edit\&redlink=1) at IDF forces in Yaroun, causing casualties. It also claimed to have launched a missile against a Merkava tank in [Netu'a](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Netu'a), while a rocket salvo targeted Israeli troops in [Al-Thaghra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Thaghra?action=edit\&redlink=1) in the outskirts of Odaisseh.[^cite_note-222]

Hezbollah fired 100 Katyusha rockets, six Falaq rockets and mortars at Metula.[^cite_note-adler-quillen-20241003-223][^cite_note-224] Hezbollah said that it targeted Safed and Kafr Giladi using rockets.[^cite_note-adler-quillen-20241003-223]

According to Hezbollah, six IDF infiltration attempts into southern Lebanon were repelled by its forces.[^cite_note-225]

#### Casualties

Two [Belgian](3343.md) journalists were wounded in Beirut.[^cite_note-226] A US resident was killed in an Israeli airstrike in Lebanon.[^cite_note-227]

The IDF announced the death of two soldiers killed in combat.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58] Hezbollah stated that it had killed 17 IDF soldiers on 3 October.[^cite_note-228]

The [World Health Organization](33583.md) said that 28 health care workers were killed in Lebanon over the past 24 hours.[^cite_note-229] Lebanese Health Minister [Firass Abiad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Firass_Abiad) said that 40 paramedics and firefighters including emergency personnel from organizations affiliated with Hezbollah were killed in Lebanon in three days.[^cite_note-40rescue-71]

#### Israeli strikes

The IAF struck Hezbollah intelligence and communications sites in Beirut.[^cite_note-230]

An Israeli strike on the municipality building in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md) killed 15 people. The IDF said that Hezbollah fighters were killed and the building was used by Hezbollah to store weapons.[^cite_note-231]

An Israeli airstrike on a building in southern Lebanon, around the area where soldiers from the [Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade) were operating, killed a Hezbollah field commander.[^cite_note-232]

An IRGC adviser died from wounds he sustained from an Israeli airstrike in Damascus three days prior.[^cite_note-233]

Personnel of the [Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade) attacked a military site where Hezbollah commanders were staying and captured the building. A commander of the Hezbollah Artillery Corps, a commander of the Hezbollah Engineering Corps and a local [Redwan Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Redwan_Force) commander were killed in the battle.[^cite_note-Hezbunits-41]

#### IDF reports

The IDF said that it struck 15 Hezbollah sites in Beirut, including weapon depots and manufacturing sites.[^cite_note-234]

In total, the IDF said it have killed more than 100 Hezbollah militants since the start of the war.[^cite_note-100milkilled-235]

#### Dahieh airstrike

Main article: [Assassination of Hashem Safieddine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Hashem_Safieddine)

Senior Hezbollah official [Hashem Safieddine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hashem_Safieddine), who was expected to succeed Nasrallah as the group's secretary-general following his assassination, was reportedly [targeted by an Israeli airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2024_Dahieh_airstrike) in Beirut.[^cite_note-236] It was unclear whether Safieddine had been killed.[^cite_note-237] Hezbollah said it had lost contact with Safieddine after the strike.[^cite_note-238]

### 4 October

#### UNIFIL

On 4 October, an international dispute occurred as the IDF ordered an Irish [UNIFIL](787766.md) peacekeeping unit deployed in southern Lebanon to abandon their border outpost.[^cite_note-239] Ireland rejected the orders, with President [Michael D. Higgins](559753.md) calling them "outrageous".[^cite_note-240]

#### Masnaa Border Crossing strike

The IDF halted traffic at the [Masnaa Border Crossing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masnaa_Border_Crossing) between Syria and Lebanon by striking "vital transportation infrastructure" using two missiles. The IDF had alleged that the border crossing was being used by Hezbollah for transferring weapons from Syria to Lebanon one day prior to the strike.[^cite_note-241]

#### Hezbollah strikes

Hezbollah said that it targeted [Haifa](55606.md) using rockets.[^cite_note-242] A rocket launched from Lebanon fell in Upper Galilee area causing a forest fire. About 50 instances of rockets or shrapnel falling were reported in [Metula](2599433.md) in the past 24 hours.[^cite_note-243] Hezbollah launched more than 100 rockets towards Israel.[^cite_note-244] Hezbollah claimed to have struck a group of Israeli soldiers in the vicinity of [Maroun al-Ras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maroun_al-Ras). Hezbollah said that it launched a missile strike on the Nafah IDF base in the Golan Heights and it also said that it targeted [Kfar Giladi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kfar_Giladi).[^cite_note-245] The IDF said that it struck Hezbollah weapons warehouses and infrastructure, including its intelligence headquarters in Beirut.[^cite_note-246]

#### Casualties

The [Lebanese National News Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_News_Agency_\(Lebanon\)) reported that four health workers were killed in an Israeli drone strike in the vicinity of a government hospital in [Marjayoun](6236310.md).[^cite_note-247]

The IDF said that over 250 Hezbollah fighters were killed in southern Lebanon since it began its ground offensive, including 21 field commanders.[^cite_note-248]

Hezbollah said that at least 20 Israeli elite soldiers were killed or wounded in clashes in a Lebanese village.[^cite_note-249]

#### Israeli control in Lebanon

Israel claimed operational control of "multiple" Shi'ite villages in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-250]



[Golani Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golani_Brigade) personnel operating in Southern Lebanon on 4 October

### 5 October

#### Hezbollah strikes

On 5 October 2024, Hezbollah claimed to have hit an Israeli Merkava tank as it was advancing in the Maroun al-Ras forest area using a guided anti-armor missile, resulting in casualties.[^cite_note-251]

Hezbollah claimed that it launched rockets targeting Israeli soldiers in Khallet Ubair village in [Yaroun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yaroun), as well as on Kafrioufel and [Kfar Giladi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kfar_Giladi) in northern Israel.[^cite_note-252]

Hezbollah said that it launched at least seven strikes on IDF soldiers including launching Fadi 1 missiles targeting [Ramat David IDF base](5340731.md) and launching rockets targeting IDF soldiers in the vicinity of the border.[^cite_note-253]

#### Israeli strikes

An Israeli strike on a mosque in [Bint Jbeil](6124393.md) also hit nearby Salah Ghandour Hospital injuring nine of its medical staff. The IDF said that it targeted Hezbollah fighters in a command center embedded inside the mosque without providing evidence.[^cite_note-254][^cite_note-255]

IDF stated that it had started pouring cement into Hezbollah tunnels to make them unusable.[^cite_note-256]

[President of France](24899.md) [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md) called for a halt on arms deliveries to Israel, which Netanyahu condemned.[^cite_note-257] In an apparent retaliation of this, the IDF blew up a gas station in Beirut owned by the French [petroleum company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Petroleum_company) [TotalEnergies](804161.md). The explosion resulted in a large fire, but no injuries were reported.[^cite_note-258][^cite_note-259]

#### Casualties

A female [Red Cross](15487.md) volunteer from [Baalbek](58207.md) died of a head injury sustained in an Israeli airstrike.[^cite_note-260] A missile hit in the vicinity of a paramedic team to prevent them from reaching the site of a bigger strike in a Beirut suburb.[^cite_note-261]

Hamas confirmed the death of Saeed Atallah Ali, one of its military officials, along with his wife and two young daughters in an Israeli drone strike in [Beddawi refugee camp](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beddawi_refugee_camp).[^cite_note-262]

A Hezbollah rocket attack lightly injured three people in [Deir al-Asad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deir_al-Asad) and caused damage in [Karmiel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karmiel).[^cite_note-263]

### 6 October

[Pope Francis](1687680.md) called for an immediate ceasefire in both Gaza and Lebanon.[^cite_note-264]

The Lebanese Education Ministry announced that 40% of the 1.25 million students in Lebanon had been displaced by the Israeli invasion and bombings.[^cite_note-265]

### 7 October

#### Israeli strikes

On 7 October, two Israeli airstrikes in towns south of [Beirut](37428.md) killed at least 12 people, including several Lebanese children.[^cite_note-266]

Israeli warplanes struck a fire station building affiliated with the Islamic Health Authority in [Baraachit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baraachit), killing ten firefighters.[^cite_note-267][^cite_note-268]

#### Hezbollah strikes

On the anniversary of the [Hamas-led attack on Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Hamas-led_attack_on_Israel), Hezbollah fired several rockets at northern Israel, wounding at least 10 people in [Haifa](55606.md) and another in [Tiberias](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tiberias). Alerts were activated in [Upper Galilee](8232648.md) after 15 rockets were detected.[^cite_note-269] The IDF announced the deaths of two soldiers in combat in the past 24 hours, on the Lebanese border, while another two were seriously injured.[^cite_note-270]

### 8 October



Israeli [Namer APC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Namer_APC) in southern Lebanon on 8 October

The IDF was filmed raising the Israeli flag in Maroun al-Ras, seemingly indicating they had captured the village.[^cite_note-271][^cite_note-272][^cite_note-273] However, according to UNIFIL, IDF troops there later withdrew from Maroun al-Ras to unknown positions.[^cite_note-274] Lebanese fishermen were forced to keep their boats ashore after Israel warned that it would expand its operations against Hezbollah to coastal areas south of [Sidon](28366.md).[^cite_note-275]

### 9 October

Hezbollah-affiliated sources stated the IDF had withdrawn from Odaisseh and Kfar Kila.[^cite_note-276] Netanyahu released an address to the government and people of Lebanon in English, saying that the country would face "destruction and suffering like we see in Gaza" if they did not rise up against Hezbollah.[^cite_note-277] IDF reported the death of one soldier during clashes in south Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

#### Derdghaya Melkite Church airstrike

See also: [2024 Derdghaya Melkite Church airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Derdghaya_Melkite_Church_airstrike)

Israel struck a church of the Melkite Greek Catholic Eparchy of Tyre that was being used to shelter displaced people in Derdghaya, killing at least eight people.[^cite_note-278]

### 10 October

#### Israeli attacks on UN peacekeepers

See also: [United Nations Interim Force in Lebanon § Attacks on UNIFIL bases](787766.md#Attacks_on_UNIFIL_bases)



Deployment of UNIFIL forces, 2018

[UNIFIL](787766.md) stated that the IDF had fired upon three peacekeeper positions, one being fired at on 9 October and two others being fired upon on 10 October. In one of the incidents, two were injured after a tank had shot at an observation tower, causing them to fall. No peacekeepers were harmed in the other two incidents.[^cite_note-279]

On the same day, Israeli troops opened fire at three UNIFIL positions in south Lebanon, including UNIFIL's main base at [Naqoura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naqoura).[^cite_note-280] The following day an IDF tank fired at the UNIFIL observation tower in Naqoura injuring two Indonesian peacekeepers. In the evening, a joint statement of condemnation was released by France, Italy and Spain, while US President Joe Biden asked Israel not to attack UNIFIL forces.[^cite_note-281]

#### Bachoura airstrike

Main article: [October 2024 Bachoura airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2024_Bachoura_airstrike)

Israeli airstrikes in two densely populated neighborhoods in Bachoura in central Beirut killed at least 22 Lebanese people and injured another 117.[^cite_note-282] Israel said it was targeting [Wafiq Safa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wafiq_Safa), Hezbollah's liaison with Lebanon's security agencies. Safa survived the attack. This was the deadliest strike on Beirut since the start of the post-2023 Israel-Hezbollah conflict, leveling an apartment building and destroying nearby cars and building interiors. A US-made munition was used in the attack.[^cite_note-283]

### 11 October

Main article: [2024 Ramyah clashes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Ramyah_clashes)

On that night, UNIFIL reported receiving "significant damage" to its buildings in Ramyah due to explosions from nearby shelling.[^cite_note-unifil-statement-12-october-2024-284]

#### Israeli attack on Lebanese military checkpoint

According to the Lebanese army, an Israeli airstrike hit a building near a military checkpoint in [Kafra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafra,_Lebanon), [Bint Jbeil District](12027489.md) in southern Lebanon, killing two LAF soldiers and wounding three other Lebanese troops.[^cite_note-285]

### 12 October

#### Israeli strikes

Nine people were killed and 24 others were wounded in Israeli strikes on the Lebanese villages of Maaysrah, in the mountains north of Beirut, and [Barja](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barja), south of Beirut.[^cite_note-286]

Israel also struck the central commercial area of [Nabatieh](43794798.md) in the south. The Lebanese Red Cross said it was initially unable to reach the area of the strike due to large fires from the blast.[^cite_note-287]

#### Gunfire and explosions near UNIFIL facilities

A UNIFIL press release stated that "Last night, a peacekeeper at UNIFIL's headquarters in [Naqoura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naqoura) was hit by gunfire due to ongoing military activity nearby." The statement also referred to buildings in their UN positions receiving "significant damage due to explosions from nearby shelling" in a separate incident in [Ramyah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ramyah).[^cite_note-unifil-statement-12-october-2024-284][^cite_note-288] A joint statement was made through the initiative of [Poland](22936.md), with 44 countries including three UN Security Council permanent members condemned the attack.[^cite_note-289]

### 13 October

#### Israeli attack on Ramyah UNIFIL post

Main article: [Israeli attack on Ramyah UNIFIL post](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_attack_on_Ramyah_UNIFIL_post)

Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu has called for UNIFIL peacekeepers to immediately withdraw from Lebanon, calling them "human shields" for Hezbollah.[^cite_note-290] Later in the day, two Israeli tanks destroyed the main gate of a UNIFIL post in [Ramyah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ramyah) and forcibly entered the post. Two hours later, the IDF fired rounds into the camp that caused smoke to rise, injuring 15 UNIFIL peacekeepers due to skin irritations and [gastrointestinal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gastrointestinal_tract) reactions.[^cite_note-bbc20241013-80] A report by one of the countries providing troops to UNIFIL said that the smoke was suspected to be [white phosphorus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_phosphorus), the use of which is unlawful in populated areas under international law.[^cite_note-291]

#### Drone attack on IDF base

Main article: [2024 Hezbollah drone strike on Binyamina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Hezbollah_drone_strike_on_Binyamina)

A Hezbollah drone attack on a gathering of IDF soldiers in Zar'it barracks in [Binyamina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Binyamina-Giv'at_Ada) killed at least four Israeli soldiers and wounded another 61.[^cite_note-292][^cite_note-IDFbaseattack-293]

### 14 October

#### Aitou airstrike

Main article: [October 2024 Aitou airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/October_2024_Aitou_airstrike)

An Israeli airstrike hit the [Maronite Christian](39667585.md) town of [Aitou](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aitou) in northern Lebanon, killing at least 21 and wounding eight.[^cite_note-294] The dead included including twelve women and two children and injured eight others.[^cite_note-295][^cite_note-296][^cite_note-297]

### 15 October

On 15 October, the [UNHCR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UNHCR) said that over 25% of Lebanon was under evacuation orders of Israel.[^cite_note-298] Israeli air strikes in [Qana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qana) killed 15 people and injured 15 others.[^cite_note-299]

The French Armed Forces Minister [Sébastien Lecornu](54364868.md) stated he supported UNIFIL remaining in place in Lebanon, despite Israel's orders for them to leave.[^cite_note-300]

#### Israeli strikes

Israel bombed homes and a healthcare center in [Qana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qana), which is notable for having previously experienced massacres in [1996](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qana_massacre) and [2006](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_Qana_airstrike). At least 10 people died in the attacks, and 54 were injured.[^cite_note-301]

### 16 October

#### Attack on Nabatieh municipal council

Main article: [Attack on Nabatieh municipal council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attack_on_Nabatieh_municipal_council)

Israel bombed a meeting of the municipal council of Nabatieh, killing at least 16 municipality staff, including the mayor Ahmad Kahil, and injuring more than 52.[^cite_note-302][^cite_note-303]

#### Hezbollah strikes

Hezbollah fired a barrage of 30 rockets towards [Karmiel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karmiel), wounding two Israelis in Upper Galilee.[^cite_note-304]

#### Israeli attack on a UNIFIL watchtower

Israeli tanks fired at a [UNIFIL](787766.md) watchtower in [Kafr Kila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kafr_Kila,_Lebanon). UNIFIL called the attack "deliberate fire on a UNIFIL position" and reported that the watchtower was damaged, along with two cameras.[^cite_note-305]

#### Israeli destruction of Mhaibib

The IDF released footage of them destroying a village. *[The Daily Telegraph](23797577.md)* reported that the village in the video was "confirmed to be [Mhaibib](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mhaibib) by local officials".[^cite_note-306] *[Al Jazeera](30204052.md)* also reported that the village was Mhaibib.[^cite_note-aljazeera20241016-307] In the same report, *Al Jazeera* published footage of soldiers they claimed were celebrating the destruction of the village, quoting the IDF as 'saying they were targeting "terrorist infrastructure."'[^cite_note-aljazeera20241016-307] *[Reuters](18998750.md)* connected an IDF statement that it had '"dismantled" a tunnel network used by Hezbollah's elite [Redwan Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Redwan_Force) in the heart of a town near the border with Israel' to the destruction of the village.[^cite_note-308] While the three outlets quoted the IDF regarding "terrorist infrastructure", they did not suggest that they had independently corroborated the IDF's allegation.

#### Casualties

Five IDF soldiers were reported killed in combat in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 17 October

The IDF confirmed that 5 soldiers had been killed in clashes with Hezbollah and 8 others were injured.[^cite_note-309]

#### UNIFIL shoots down armed drone

A [German warship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_corvette_Ludwigshafen_am_Rhein) operating as a part of UNIFIL shot down a drone of unknown origin in the [Mediterranean Sea](19006.md) about 30 kilometres (19 mi) northwest of the Lebanese port of [Naqoura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naqoura).[^cite_note-310] According to the German defense ministry, there was no damage to the warship and no injuries to its crew.[^cite_note-311]

### 18 October

The IDF reported that an Israeli soldier died from wounds suffered in a 9 October attack in south Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 19 October

#### Sahel Alma airstrike

On 19 October 2024, the Israel Defense Forces conducted an airstrike on a car in the village of [Sahel Alma](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sahel_Alma), part of the city of [Jounieh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jounieh), as part of the 2024 Israeli invasion of Lebanon.[^cite_note-OLJ-312] An Israeli drone targeted a Honda sports utility vehicle driving on the main highway from Byblos to Jounieh in Sahel Alma. The drone hit the car causing it to lose control before it stopped 100 meters down the highway where a man and woman, reported to be husband and wife, ran out of the car and into a field on the side of the highway before being killed in another blast.[^cite_note-OLJ-312][^cite_note-Arabiya-313][^cite_note-314] This was the first time that the predominantly Christian city of [Jounieh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jounieh) was struck since the war began.[^cite_note-OLJ-312][^cite_note-315][^cite_note-316] A witness saw the charred remains of one of the persons in the field.[^cite_note-Arabiya-313] It was reported that there were three rocket strikes in the attack.[^cite_note-OLJ-312]

A member of the Jounieh municipality, Fadi Fayad, stated that "It seems they were coming from Jbeil toward Jounieh at the time of the strike...We urge citizens to be vigilant and responsible and to inform the municipality of any new leasing operations (new individuals renting apartments) to maintain the security of the area, as no area can be considered safe."[^cite_note-OLJ-312] The [Lebanese National News Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_News_Agency_\(Lebanon\)) has referred to the two killed as martyrs.[^cite_note-317]

#### Drone attack on Benjamin Netanyahu's residence

Main article: [2024 drone attack on Benjamin Netanyahu's residence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_drone_attack_on_Benjamin_Netanyahu's_residence)

A drone attack believed to have originated from Lebanon was made on the residence of Benjamin Netanyahu in the resort town [Caesarea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Caesarea_\(modern_town\)). Netanyahu was not in the residence at the time, and no casualties were reported.[^cite_note-318] In response, Netanyahu stated that "the proxies of Iran who today tried to assassinate me and my wife made a bitter mistake."[^cite_note-319] Iran has attributed the reported attack to Hezbollah, with the state-run [IRNA news agency](841314.md) quoting Iran's mission to the UN saying: "The action in question has been carried out by Hezbollah in Lebanon."[^cite_note-320]

### 20 October

#### Destruction of observation tower in Marwahin

UNIFIL released a press statement stating that the IDF deliberately bulldozed a UNIFIL observation tower and perimeter fence in the Lebanese border town of [Marwahin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marwahin).[^cite_note-321]

#### IDF strike on LAF soldiers

An IDF airstrike killed three LAF soldiers driving in a vehicle near an IDF area of operation.[^cite_note-322][^cite_note-323]

#### Hezbollah rocket attack



Interception of missiles over northern Israel

About 200 missiles were fired at northern Israel.[^cite_note-324][^cite_note-325]

### 21 October

A bomb hit a residential building across the street from the Rafik Hariri University hospital, killing at least 18, including four children, and injuring 60.[^cite_note-cnn20241102-326]

#### Clashes

Hezbollah and the IDF engaged in clashes in the villages of [Ayta ash Shab](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayta_ash_Shab) and Ramiya as the IDF attempted to advance into the villages.[^cite_note-327]

### 22 October

An Israeli airstrike on a house in [Teffahta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teffahta?action=edit\&redlink=1) killed 19 people.[^cite_note-328]

#### Hezbollah rocket barrage

Hezbollah conducted a rocket barrage on [Neot Mordechai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neot_Mordechai) in northern Israel, a 27-year-old IDF reservist from Tel Aviv was killed and three other soldiers were seriously wounded.[^cite_note-329] Another soldier was killed in south Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 23 October

Air attacks on Dahieh resumed in what was called one of the worst nights of bombing in the neighborhood to date. Six buildings, including the offices of [Al Mayadeen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Mayadeen), were destroyed in seventeen raids.[^cite_note-330]

#### Casualties

The IDF said that four of its soldiers were killed in southern Lebanon, while six others were evacuated from Lebanon after being seriously wounded.[^cite_note-331] Hezbollah said that its fighters have killed more than 70 Israeli soldiers.[^cite_note-aljazeera20241024-332]

### 24 October

#### Ayta al-Shaab clashes

Hezbollah reported that its fighters were engaged in ongoing "intense" clashes against IDF vehicles in Ayta al-Shaab. On the same day, Hezbollah said that it destroyed an Israeli tank with a guided missile, killing and wounding the soldiers inside.[^cite_note-aljazeera20241024-332]

#### Military casualties

Five IDF soldiers were killed and 19 others were wounded in a southern Lebanon village. According to the IDF, Hezbollah forces attacked the building in which the soldiers were accepting logistics supplies.[^cite_note-TOI25O-333]

### 25 October

Three journalists were killed in an Israeli airstrike on a residential compound housing media workers in [Hasbaya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hasbaya). Two were employees of [Al Mayadeen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Mayadeen), and the other worked for [Al Manar TV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Manar).[^cite_note-334]

### 26 October

The IDF said five of its soldiers were killed in southern Lebanon,[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58] bringing the IDF's reported casualties up to 32 since 1 October, according to [CNN](62028.md).[^cite_note-cnn20241027-335]

#### IDF attack on UNIFIL observation post near Dhayra on 22 October

A UNIFIL press release on 25 October stated that IDF soldiers fired at an observation post near the town of [Dhayra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dhayra?action=edit\&redlink=1) on 22 October 2024.[^cite_note-336][^cite_note-337]

### 27 October

At least eight people were killed and 25 wounded in an Israeli strike on the Haret Saida neighoorhood of Sidon.[^cite_note-cnn20241027-335]

### 28 October

#### Beqaa Valley airstrikes

Main article: [2024 Beqaa Valley airstrikes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Beqaa_Valley_airstrikes)

The Lebanese Health Ministry reported that at least 60 people,[^cite_note-theguardian20241104-338] including two children, were killed and 58 wounded in strikes on 12 areas of the [Beqaa Valley](2162385.md). Israel bombed the southern towns of Rachaya al-Fakhar, Kfar Hamam, Alma ash-Shaab, and Naqoura with artillery shells.[^cite_note-339]

### 29 October

On 29 October, nine people were killed and a girl was trapped under rubble in an Israeli airstrike in Haret Saida, southeast of Sidon.[^cite_note-340][^cite_note-341] An Israeli airstrike in [Sarafand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sarafand,_Lebanon) killed 10 people and injured 21 others.[^cite_note-342][^cite_note-343] Israeli defense minister [Yoav Gallant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoav_Gallant) claimed that Hezbollah maintained only 20% of the rockets and missiles it initially had before the conflict.[^cite_note-344] Hezbollah denied the claim.[^cite_note-345] The IDF said that its military goals in Lebanon had been achieved with the Israeli government planning to go ahead with diplomatic negotiations.[^cite_note-y547-346] Israeli officials said the ceasefire negotiations were "in advanced stages" as White House special envoy [Amos Hochstein](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amos_Hochstein) traveled to the region to mediate the negotiations.[^cite_note-g405-347]

An IDF soldier died of wounds suffered during battle the previous days.[^cite_note-348]

### 30 October

Israeli struck [Baalbek](58207.md), killing 19 people, including eight women. 20 strikes were reported in the area, five of which were in the city limits.[^cite_note-bbc20241031-349][^cite_note-theguardian20241104-338]

The IDF claimed to have killed dozens of militants one day prior.[^cite_note-350] Israeli airstrikes in [Sohmor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sohmor) killed 11 people and injured 15 others.[^cite_note-351] Israeli strikes killed 19 people, including eight women in two towns in [Baalbek District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baalbek_District).[^cite_note-bbc20241031-349][^cite_note-352]

### 31 October

On 31 October, [SANA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SANA) reported that Israeli strikes hit a number of residential buildings in [Al-Qusayr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Qusayr,_Syria), causing "material damage" to its industrial zone and some of its residential neighborhoods killing 10 people, including civilians.[^cite_note-353][^cite_note-354] The IDF said that it struck Hezbollah command centers and weapon depots.[^cite_note-355]

Hezbollah fired rockets at the Haifa region, killing two Israelis, while four Thai workers and one Israeli national were killed in Metula.[^cite_note-356]

### 1 November

At least 52 Lebanese people were killed and 72 wounded in Israeli airstrikes in northeastern Lebanon.[^cite_note-357]

An Israeli airstrike on a home in [Amhaz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amhaz?action=edit\&redlink=1) killed at least 12 people and injured several others.[^cite_note-358][^cite_note-359]

### 2 November

#### Israeli kidnapping

Main article: [November 2024 Batroun raid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/November_2024_Batroun_raid)

The IDF claimed it had killed a commander of the [Nasser Unit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nasser_Unit?action=edit\&redlink=1) rocket unit of Hezbollah, Jaafar Khader Faoura,[^cite_note-360] and captured an unnamed "senior Hezbollah operative" from northern Lebanon and transported them into Israeli territory. The Lebanese government stated that it would file a complaint to the [United Nations Security Council](31956.md) in response to the abduction.[^cite_note-361]

#### Hezbollah rocket attack

A rocket fired by Hezbollah injured 19 people in Tayibe in central Israel.[^cite_note-362]

### 3 November

At least three people were killed and nine wounded in an Israeli strike in Haret Saida.[^cite_note-363][^cite_note-364]

### 5 November

An Israeli strike on a residential building in [Barja](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barja) killed at least 30 people and injured several others including a woman and her child.[^cite_note-365]

An IDF soldier died from wounds suffered in the north.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 7 November

UNIFIL confirmed that five of its Malaysian peacekeepers were injured by an Israeli airstrike on [Sidon](28366.md).[^cite_note-366][^cite_note-UNIFIL_LA-367] This was followed by a statement from the Lebanese Army who confirmed that three of its soldiers were also injured in the same attack.[^cite_note-UNIFIL_LA-367] The attack was heavily condemned by [France](5843419.md), which called for an immediate cessation of hostilities and the complete implementation of [UN Security Council Resolution 1701](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UN_Security_Council_Resolution_1701) to enable displaced people in Israel and Lebanon to safely return home.[^cite_note-368]

### 8 November

An IDF soldier died from wounds sustained on 26 October.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 10 November

An Israeli airstrike in Aalmat village, Lebanon, killed at least 23 people, including seven children, and injured six others.[^cite_note-369]

An Israeli strike on a civil defense center affiliated to the Islamic Mission Scouts Association in [Ras al-Ain, Lebanon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ras_al-Ain,_Lebanon) killed 15 people and injured two others.[^cite_note-370][^cite_note-371]

### 11 November

An Israeli airstrike on a building in [Ain Yaaqoub](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ain_Yaaqoub) killed at least 14 people, injured 15 others and trapped some people under the rubble.[^cite_note-372]

### 12 November

An Israeli air strike in [Joun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joun) killed 15 people including eight women and four children and wounded 12 others.[^cite_note-373]

### 13 November

Six IDF soldiers from the Golani Brigade were killed in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 14 November

The IDF struck a building in the vicinity of [Beirut International Airport](12869180.md), and footage shared by Lebanese media showed a passenger plane being taxied on the runway near the strike.[^cite_note-374] An Israeli airstrike on a civil defense center in [Duris, Lebanon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Duris,_Lebanon) killed at least 15 people.[^cite_note-375][^cite_note-376][^cite_note-377] One IDF platoon commander of the Golani Brigade was killed in Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 15 November

Twelve paramedics were killed in an Israeli airstrike on Duris.[^cite_note-378] One IDF soldier of the Golani Brigade was killed in south Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 16 November

Israeli troops reached the deepest point in Lebanon since the start of the invasion. They briefly captured a hill in [Chamaa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chamaa) and according to Lebanese state media, destroyed the Shrine of [Shamoun Al Safa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saint_Peter). Israeli warplanes attacked Dahiyeh, wounding footballer [Celine Haidar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Celine_Haidar), and also attacked Tyre. A strike on [Khreibeh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khreibeh) killed a family of six.[^cite_note-379][^cite_note-MundoDeportivo-380][^cite_note-381]

### 19 November

[UNICEF](19698110.md) spokesperson James Elder stated that more than 200 children have been killed since the start of the Israeli invasion.[^cite_note-382][^cite_note-383][^cite_note-aljazeera20241119-384] One IDF soldier was killed in south Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 20 November

Three IDF soldiers were killed in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-IDFlossesgov-58]

### 22 November

Lebanon's health ministry stated that a hospital director and six others were killed by an Israeli airstrike in northeastern Lebanon, and a separate Israeli airstrike killed five paramedics in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-apnews20241123-69]

### 23 November

Main article: [2024 Basta airstrikes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Basta_airstrikes)

[An Israeli airstrike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Basta_airstrikes) on a building in Basta area of [Beirut Central District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beirut_Central_District) targeting Hezbollah official [Muhammad Haydar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad_Haydar) killed at least 29 people and injured 67 others.[^cite_note-385][^cite_note-386][^cite_note-387] The target was reportedly not present in the building.[^cite_note-388]

### 26 November

IDF troops reached the [Litani River](1920925.md) hours before Israel's security cabinet reached [a ceasefire](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Israel%E2%80%93Lebanon_ceasefire_agreement) with [Hezbollah](13919.md).[^cite_note-389] The ceasefire stipulates that Hezbollah's militants would leave the border with Israel in exchange for the withdrawal of Israeli troops from southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-390][^cite_note-391]

### 27 November

The IDF estimated that it killed several dozens of Hezbollah operatives in its strikes on a Radwan Force base in [Beqaa Valley](2162385.md) in the night leading up to the truce.[^cite_note-392] The ceasefire deal between Israel and Hezbollah went into effect at 4\:00 a.m. [UTC+02\:00](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UTC+02\:00).[^cite_note-393]

## Post-ceasefire events

### 28 November 2024

Despite the ceasefire being in effect, Israel continued its offensive, with at least 18 strikes as of the evening of 28 November,[^cite_note-394] in one case citing the presence of "several suspects" in vehicles arriving in southern Lebanon.[^cite_note-395] The IDF opened fire on at least six targets, with the Lebanese Army stating that the IDF was in violation of the ceasefire. Details on the alleged suspects were not released, though the IDF said it was launching strikes against "terrorist activity."[^cite_note-396] Additionally, the IDF told Lebanese citizens not to move south of the Litani River, establishing a night-time curfew from 5\:00pm local time until 7\:00am the next day.[^cite_note-397]

### 29 November

Despite the ceasefire being in effect, the IDF continued its offensive into Khiam.[^cite_note-398]

### 2 December

Hezbollah launched two mortars towards [Shebaa Farms](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shebaa_Farms), claiming that it was responding defensively to repeated ceasefire violations by the IDF.[^cite_note-399] In response, the IDF struck dozens of Hezbollah members and rocket launchers, including the one used in the mortar attack.[^cite_note-400] Six people were killed in [Haris](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haris,_Bint_Jbeil) and four others in [Talloussah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Talloussah).[^cite_note-401][^cite_note-402]

### 9 December

Four IDF soldiers were killed in a tunnel explosion during an operational detonation of a Hezbollah cache in south Lebanon.[^cite_note-TOI09122024-403]

### 17 December

An Israeli drone targeted a car in Majdal Zun injuring two. Israel also demolished homes in Naqoura.[^cite_note-MEM-Dec17-404]

The town of Kfar Kila was completely demolished by the IDF while Halta and Shebaa were targeted by Israeli artillery.[^cite_note-MEM-Dec17-404]

According to the [Middle East Monitor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_East_Monitor), a Qatari-financed not-for-profit [press monitoring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Media_monitoring_service) organization and [lobbying group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lobbying_group), Israel had violated the ceasefire 12 times, bringing the total number of violations to 248, with casualties post-ceasefire numbering 30 dead and 36 wounded, as of 17 December.[^cite_note-MEM-Dec17-404]

### 18 December

A group of [Israeli settlers](15123.md) of the far-right [Uri Tzafon Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uri_Tzafon_Movement?action=edit\&redlink=1) attempted to cross the Blue Line and establish a settlement in the Lebanese village of Maroun al-Ras but were dispersed by the IDF, as the area was a closed military zone. The IDF described the situation as a "grave incident", and emphasized the risks of uncoordinated civilian movement into Lebanon.[^cite_note-405]

### 20 December 2024

Israel violated the ceasefire 6 times, flying drones near Tyre and bombarding Alma al-Shaab.[^cite_note-406]

### 2025

See also: [2025 in Lebanon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_in_Lebanon)

#### 3 January 2025

The UN called on Israel to withdrawal from southern Lebanon in accordance to the ceasefire, which they have yet to do.[^cite_note-407]

#### 4 January

The IDF announced that it planned to renew its offensive for at least 30 days after the 60 day ceasefire runs out claiming that they will seek to occupy all territory south of the Litani River, should the Lebanese army not evict Hezbollah.[^cite_note-408]

#### 10 January

An Israeli drone-strike on [Tayr Dibba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tayr_Dibba) killed 5.[^cite_note-409]

#### 26 January

See also: [January 2025 southern Lebanon attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/January_2025_southern_Lebanon_attack)

The ceasefire's deadline for the withdrawal of Israeli forces from Lebanon was due to expire on 26 January. The Israeli forces still remained in Lebanon at this time and the ceasefire was extended to mid-February. Israel accused the Lebanese government of failing to remove Hezbollah forces from the area as stipulated by the ceasefire agreement. The Lebanese government in turn blamed Israel for not withdrawing by the agreed deadline.[^cite_note-410]

Lebanon’s Ministry of Public Health stated that Israeli soldiers attacked Lebanese civilians who attempted to return to towns still occupied by Israeli forces.[^cite_note-411] AP reported that 24 people were killed on that day and two more on the next day.[^cite_note-412] According to the IDF, its forces opened fire on suspects who approached Israeli troops and posed "imminent threat."[^cite_note-toi2701-413]

#### 8 February

Six people were killed and two others were wounded in Israeli [airstrikes](323167.md) near [Jannata](52217649.md), [South Governorate](1283570.md).[^cite_note-414]

#### 14 February

The deputy commander of UNIFIL was injured after his convoy was attacked by Hezbollah supporters after two planes from Iran were barred from landing in Beirut.[^cite_note-415]

#### 17 February

Despite the ceasefire agreement giving an extended deadline for Israeli forces to leave Lebanon by 18 February 2025, the Israeli military announced on 17 February that it would retain and maintain five military outposts on hills and mountains in southern Lebanon near the Israeli communities of [Shlomi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shlomi,_Israel), [Zar'it](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zar'it), [Avivim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avivim), [Margaliot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Margaliot) and [Metula](2599433.md).[^cite_note-416][^cite_note-b407-417][^cite_note-418]

#### 18 February

Lebanese state media reported that Israeli forces mostly left Lebanon, except for five Israeli military outposts in Lebanon.[^cite_note-o999-419] The Lebanese government stated it would not tolerate IDF troops remaining in Lebanon as it would be an "occupation".[^cite_note-b407-417]

#### 12 April

According to reports Hezbollah has largely dismantled its military presence in southern Lebanon, handing over control of 190 military positions out of 265 to the Lebanese army.[^cite_note-middleeasteye20250412-420] The move complies with a US-brokered ceasefire agreement from November 2024, which requires Hezbollah to reposition north of the Litani River and deploy around 5,000 Lebanese troops to the south.[^cite_note-421] Intended to ease tensions along the Israel-Lebanon border and support the return of displaced residents, the withdrawal involved removing heavy weaponry, though some local fighters remain lightly armed. International observers continue to monitor the fragile situation to ensure adherence to the agreement.[^cite_note-middleeasteye20250412-420]

#### 16 April

Israeli defense minister Israel Katz on 16 April 2025 declared that Israeli forces would indefinitely remain in "security zones" that they "cleared and seized" in Lebanon, Gaza and Syria, as "a buffer between the enemy and \[Israeli] communities".[^cite_note-422] At the time, while Israeli forces had departed from villages in Lebanon, Israeli forces continued their presence in "five strategic overlook locations inside Lebanon", along the Israel-Lebanon border, reported the Associated Press.[^cite_note-423]

#### September

See also: [2025 Bint Jbeil drone strike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Bint_Jbeil_drone_strike)

#### November

See also: [2025 Sidon airstrike](81633803.md) and [November 2025 Israeli attack in Beirut](81670215.md)

## Impacts

### Lebanese healthcare

According to a CNN analysis of verified Lebanese Health Ministry data, between 23 September and 23 October, Israeli airstrikes damaged 34 hospitals, hit 107 ambulances, and killed 111 [emergency medical technicians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emergency_medical_technician) (EMTs) in Lebanon. The analysis also found that Israel had dropped bombs within "lethal proximity", 340 metres (1,120 ft), of at least 19 hospitals and within 500 metres (1,600 ft) of 24 hospitals.[^cite_note-cnn20241102-326]

According to the Lebanese health ministry, Israeli strikes targeted 40 hospitals, 84 medical and ambulance centers, and a total of 243 medical facilities, killing at least 178 Lebanese medical staff members since October 2023.[^cite_note-cnn20241101-424]

The [World Health Organization](33583.md) (WHO) reported that it would be unable to "deliver a large shipment of trauma and medical supplies" originally scheduled for October 4 to Lebanon due to the almost complete closure of Beirut's airport.[^cite_note-washingtonpost20241003-87] In a statement released on 16 October, the WHO stated that "\[Israeli] attacks on hospitals and health workers jeopardize provision of health in Lebanon," adding that "out of 207 primary health care centres and dispensaries in conflict-affected areas, 100 are now closed."[^cite_note-425]

Early in the invasion, the near total closure of Beirut's airport prevented medical aid supply deliveries from the WHO.[^cite_note-washingtonpost20241003-87]

During the invasion, hospitals in Lebanon have experienced an increase in patients with miscarriages, early births, and other pregnancy complications, as people have been forced to flee their homes.[^cite_note-426]

At least one case of [cholera](7591.md) has been confirmed in Lebanon, emerging from the poor sanitation and water conditions those displaced have been living in. The crowded shelters and public schools used to house the displaced population combined with the strained healthcare system have prompted fears of communicable disease outbreaks.[^cite_note-427]

## Casualties

Since mid-September 2024, Israeli strikes on Lebanon have killed at least 2,267 people and injured 11,022 more, according to a [CNN](62028.md) tally.[^cite_note-cnn20241101-424] While in Mid-November 2024 Lebanon Ministry of Public Health reported 3,583 killed, including 231 children.[^cite_note-428] [UNICEF](19698110.md) confirmed that more than 200 children has been killed.[^cite_note-aljazeera20241119-384] The Lebanese Health Ministry has reported that most Lebanese killed by Israel are civilians.[^cite_note-barrons20241106-65][^cite_note-timep20241114-106][^cite_note-429]

The Hezbollah leader [Naim Qassem](28105310.md) said that 5,000 Hezbollah fighters were killed and 13,000 were wounded in the war.[^cite_note-qassem_casualties-72]

Additionally, at least 1.2 million Lebanese civilians have been displaced as a result of Israeli bombings and evacuation orders, which the [United Nations](31769.md) described as "the largest wave of displacement Lebanon has seen in decades".[^cite_note-washingtonpost20241003-87]

## War crimes

### By Israel

See also: [Israeli war crimes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_war_crimes)

[Human Rights Watch](46530.md) states that it has documented "flagrant violations of the laws of war and war crimes by the Israeli military, including apparently deliberate or indiscriminate attacks on journalists, civilians, medics, financial institutions, and peacekeepers, in addition to the widespread and unlawful use of white phosphorus in populated areas, among other violations."[^cite_note-430] Twenty NGOs have called on the UN members states of the [UN Human Rights Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UN_Human_Rights_Council) to convene a Special Session on the situation in Lebanon to investigate all violations of humanitarian and human rights law.[^cite_note-431]

#### Killing of civilians in Lebanon

On 5 November 2023, an Israeli airstrike hit a car near Ainata, Lebanon, killing three children and their grandmother, and injuring their mother. The IDF admitted to striking the vehicle.[^cite_note-APNews_Ainata-432] [Human Rights Watch](46530.md) stated that their killings should be investigated as an apparent war crime.[^cite_note-HRW_Ainata-433] [Najib Mikati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Najib_Mikati), Lebanon's caretaker prime minister, called the attack a "heinous crime" and said that Lebanon would file a complaint to the UN Security Council.[^cite_note-APNews_Ainata-432] According to the Syrian Observatory for Human Rights, at least 78 Syrian civilians were killed by the IDF.[^cite_note-434]

#### Attacks on Lebanese healthcare facilities and healthcare workers

See also: [Medical neutrality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medical_neutrality)

According to *Al Jazeera*, Israel has "carried out more than 280 attacks on emergency medical facilities in Lebanon" in the first 13 months of the war. This has resulted in the deaths of at least 208 health care workers, and 311 other injuries.[^cite_note-435] Targeting health establishments and units, including hospitals and their medical staff, is [illegal under international humanitarian law](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Geneva_Convention).[^cite_note-436] Human Rights Watch states that it has documented three Israeli attacks on Lebanese medical workers and healthcare facilities and refers to the attacks as "apparent war crimes."[^cite_note-437]

Israel justified the strikes as targeting Hezbollah bunkers beneath the hospitals,[^cite_note-438] as medical facilities lose protections when they are used for militant activity.

#### Attacks on civilian targets

In October 2024, Israel bombed financial institutions it stated were affiliated with Hezbollah, leading [Amnesty International](18947898.md) to state the strikes should be investigated as possible war crimes.[^cite_note-439]

#### Attacks on journalists

[Reporters without Borders](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reporters_without_Borders) called for an independent investigation into a "possible war crime" in response to an Israeli airstrike that hit a guesthouse in the town of [Hasbaya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hasbaya) killing and injuring several journalists.[^cite_note-440]

#### Attacks on UNIFIL positions

In October 2024, Israel attacked the [UNIFIL peacekeeping bases](787766.md#Attacks_on_UNIFIL_bases) in southern Lebanon. In response, UNIFL stated, "Any deliberate attack on peacekeepers is a grave violation of international humanitarian law and of Security Council resolution 1701".[^cite_note-441] According to the Israeli Ambassador to the UN, "Hezbollah terrorists are using UNIFIL outposts as hiding places and as places of ambushes."[^cite_note-442] Human Rights Watch has called for a UN inquiry on the Israeli military's repeated attacks on UNIFIL's peacekeepers.[^cite_note-443]

### By Hezbollah

#### Attacks on civilian targets

Hezbollah repeatedly announced intentions to launch inaccurate rockets at civilian cities, per Amnesty International reporting. Amnesty characterized the attacks as "attacking the civilian city or town".[^cite_note-amnesty20241220-444] Amnesty further describes the rockets being used by Hezbollah as "inherently inaccurate, as it is not possible to aim them with any degree of confidence or determine exactly where they will strike."

#### Killing of civilians in Israel

An Amnesty International investigation reported 3 cases of Hezbollah rockets that targeted civilian areas in Israel in October 2024. 8 civilians were killed and at least 16 more were injured.[^cite_note-amnesty20241220-444] The deaths included Mohammed Naim, a 23-year-old [Arab Israeli](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_Israeli).[^cite_note-445]

#### Attacks on UNIFIL positions

In October 2024, Hezbollah attacked the [UNIFIL peacekeeping headquarters](787766.md#Attacks_on_UNIFIL_bases) in Naquora, Lebanon.[^cite_note-aljazeera20241029-446] UNIFIL reported "“A rocket hit UNIFIL’s headquarters in Naqoura, setting a vehicle workshop on fire, \[which] was fired from north of UNIFIL’s headquarters, likely by Hezbollah or an affiliated group”[^cite_note-aljazeera20241029-446] Eight Austrian peacekeepers were injured.

## Reactions

### Lebanese government
