The government of Lebanon and the [Lebanese Armed Forces](17779.md) (LAF) issued a statement stating they would not be getting involved in the conflict between Israel and Hezbollah, following a meeting between [Commander of the Lebanese Armed Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commander_of_the_Lebanese_Armed_Forces) [Joseph Aoun](53467128.md) and [Speaker of Parliament](30862356.md) [Nabih Berri](592775.md).[^cite_note-Lebanon_Government_Response-11] The LAF withdrew some personnel from observation posts along the blue line, however, despite this, they have given no intention of withdrawing from territory south of the [Litani River](1920925.md) and have stated they will "respond to Israeli fire on its positions."[^cite_note-Lebanon_Government_Response-11][^cite_note-reuters20240929-105] [Prime Minister of Lebanon](508949.md), [Najib Mikati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Najib_Mikati), has offered to deploy the LAF along the Blue Line if Hezbollah withdraws to the Litani River.[^cite_note-Foreign_Policy-447][^cite_note-La_Croix-448]

*[Foreign Policy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_Policy)* reported that "Lebanese and Western military officials as well as local politicians and notables" stated the LAF would stay out of the conflict "as long as it can" due to concerns that the LAF doesn't have the capability to win, or even credibly participate in fighting as the LAF only numbers 70,000 soldiers, many of whom also work other jobs, lacks any fighter jets and only owns outdated tanks.[^cite_note-Foreign_Policy-447] Instead the LAF is focusing on being a "police force" to keep the various internal factions within Lebanon at peace while Hezbollah and Israel fight.[^cite_note-Foreign_Policy-447][^cite_note-La_Croix-448] Additionally, the LAF has no intention of siding with Hezbollah, as that would cause all western arms shipments to the LAF to cease, since Hezbollah is recognized by the LAF's arms suppliers as a terrorist organization.[^cite_note-Foreign_Policy-447][^cite_note-449] However, the LAF is at risk of losing the position of "the only legitimate defender of Lebanon" to Hezbollah should it remain out of the conflict, which would only strengthen Hezbollah's internal support within Lebanon.[^cite_note-Foreign_Policy-447]

### International community

-  [Canada](5042916.md): Started evacuating its nationals from Lebanon by reserving 800 passenger seats on available commercial flights. Foreign Minister [Mélanie Joly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/M%C3%A9lanie_Joly) said that "The security situation in Lebanon is becoming increasingly dangerous and volatile".

  [^cite_note-bfmtv-450]

-  [China](5405.md): The Spokesperson of the [Foreign Ministry](7453497.md) stated that China opposes "the violation of Lebanon's sovereignty, security and territorial integrity."

  [^cite_note-PRC1-451]

  &#x20;China's embassy in Beirut announced on 2 October that it had evacuated over 200 Chinese citizens and their family members from Lebanon to China and [Cyprus](5593.md).

  [^cite_note-452]

-  [Colombia](5222.md): The Foreign Ministry organized the evacuation of 117 Colombians from Lebanon in Beirut by [Colombia's Air Force](6719810.md) while it was delivering humanitarian aide on 2 October.

  [^cite_note-453]

  &#x20;In a statement on the evacuations, the Foreign Ministry "reiterate\[d] Colombia's rejection of any military action against innocent citizens and call\[ed] for an immediate ceasefire".

  [^cite_note-454]

-  [Denmark](76972.md): [Prime Minister](273720.md) [Mette Frederiksen](26040044.md) stated that she supports an international intervention to support the [two-state solution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Two-state_solution) and end the conflict.

  [^cite_note-455]

-  [France](5843419.md): Deployed a [naval warship](383256.md) off the coast of Lebanon to aid in the evacuation of its nationals,

  [^cite_note-bfmtv-450]

  &#x20;as well as sent humanitarian aid to Lebanon with the plane used on the way back to evacuate French citizens.

  [^cite_note-Franceinfo-456]

  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Jean-Noël Barrot](54388162.md) traveled to Lebanon two days prior to the start of the invasion, stating France "stands with Lebanon" ahead of a war "it did not choose".

  [^cite_note-457]

  &#x20;On 8 October, he called Israeli Prime Minister [Benjamin Netanyahu](56472.md)'s rhetoric on Israel's military operations in Lebanon a "provocation".

  [^cite_note-Franceinfo-456]

-  [Ireland](14560.md): President [Michael D. Higgins](559753.md) criticized Israel's actions as "outrageous" after it ordered an Irish UNIFIL peacekeeping unit to abandon their outpost in southern Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-458]

-  [Italy](14532.md): [Foreign Minister](3051160.md) [Antonio Tajani](1767298.md) announced that Italy would be willing to send troops to the UN for the establishment of a Palestinian state due to instability caused by the invasion.

  [^cite_note-459]

-  [Jordan](7515964.md): [Deputy Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deputy_Prime_Minister) and [Minister of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minister_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Jordan\)) [Ayman Safadi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayman_Safadi) talked to Lebanese prime minister [Najib Mikati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Najib_Mikati) and expressed Jordan's support for Lebanese sovereignty.

  [^cite_note-460]

  &#x20;Additionally, [Abdullah II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdullah_II_of_Jordan) directed two aid planes to Lebanon.

-  [NATO](21133.md): Secretary-General [Mark Rutte](4321056.md) expressed hope that "hostilities will end as soon as possible".

  [^cite_note-aljazeera-01-10-24-461]

-  [Oman](22316.md): The [Foreign Ministry](36308453.md) released a statement saying that it "expresses the Sultanate of Oman's condemnation and denunciation of the raids launched by the Israeli occupation forces on Lebanese territory." It also iterated Oman's "solidarity with the sisterly Lebanese Republic" and its "complete rejection of any violations of Lebanon's sovereignty and territory".

  [^cite_note-462]

-  [Palestinian Authority](24093.md): Palestinian President [Mahmoud Abbas](232595.md) accused Israel of "genocide".

  [^cite_note-463]

-  [Qatar](8238258.md): [Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_prime_ministers_of_Qatar) [Sheikh Mohammed bin Abdulrahman Al Thani](49695374.md) held phone calls with Lebanon's army chief and prime minister expressing "deep concern" over the situation.

  [^cite_note-464]

  &#x20;Minister for international cooperation, [Lolwah Al-Khater](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lolwah_Al-Khater) condemned the attack, stating on [X](9988187.md) that "A monster has been unleashed in our region".

  [^cite_note-465]

-  [Russia](25391.md): [Kremlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kremlin) spokesman [Dmitry Peskov](9214683.md) stated that Moscow is concerned with the recent escalation in Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-466]

  &#x20;The [Foreign Ministry](4432213.md) condemned Israel's invasion and called on Israel to "immediately cease hostilities, withdraw their troops from Lebanese territory and engage in a real search for peaceful ways to resolve the Middle East conflict."

  [^cite_note-467]

-  [Turkey](11125639.md): The [Foreign Ministry](10283896.md) stated the attack violates Lebanon's sovereignty.

  [^cite_note-468]

  &#x20;President [Recep Tayyip Erdoğan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recep_Tayyip_Erdo%C4%9Fan), accused Israel of "genocide".

  [^cite_note-469]

-  [United Arab Emirates](69328.md): The [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](53793712.md) stated that it held "deep concern" about the situation, that it had "reaffirmed its unwavering position towards the unity of Lebanon, national sovereignty, and territorial integrity", and that [President](9480572.md) [Mohamed bin Zayed Al Nahyan](2121945.md) had "directed the delivery of an urgent \$100m relief package to the people of Lebanon".

  [^cite_note-aljazeera-01-10-24-461]

-  [United Kingdom](31717.md): [Foreign Secretary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_Secretary_\(United_Kingdom\)) [David Lammy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Lammy) stated the UK had chartered a commercial flight for its citizens wishing to leave Lebanon and that the flight would leave on 2 October from [Beirut–Rafic Hariri International Airport](12869180.md) adding that "The safety of British citizens in Lebanon is our absolute priority".

  [^cite_note-bfmtv-450]

  &#x20;Prior to the start of the invasion, Lammy had called for a "an immediate ceasefire" and "a political solution".

  [^cite_note-470]

-  [United States](3434750.md): During a 30 September press conference, President [Joe Biden](145422.md) responded to a reporter's question on Israel's actions in Lebanon saying he was aware of Israel's plans, but "comfortable with them stopping", adding "we should have a ceasefire now."

  [^cite_note-471]

  &#x20;On 1 October however, Defense Secretary [Lloyd Austin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lloyd_Austin) told Israeli Defense Minister [Yoav Gallant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoav_Gallant) that the United States supported Israel's ground offensive.

  [^cite_note-aljazeera-01-10-24-461]

  &#x20;On 7 October, State Department spokesperson [Matthew Miller](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Miller_\(spokesperson\)) said the United States supports Israel's "ability to target militants, to degrade Hezbollah's infrastructure, to degrade Hezbollah's capability".

  [^cite_note-472]

-  [European Union](9317.md): On 14 October, Spain and Ireland requested the [European Commission](9974.md) to suspend the [EU–Israel Association Agreement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israel%E2%80%93European_Union_relations#The_EU%E2%80%93Israel_Association_Agreement_\(2000\)) as human rights (article 2 of the Agreement) are being violated by Israel due to its invasion of Lebanon.

  [^cite_note-473]

### Evacuations



[President Lula da Silva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_Lula_da_Silva) receiving repatriated Brazilian nationals from Lebanon on 6 October 2024. Brazil has the highest number of [Lebanese descendants and expatriates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lebanese_diaspora) in the world.

Since the escalation of the conflict between Israel and Hezbollah, many countries have called on their citizens to leave Lebanon and/or started evacuating them such as Australia, Canada, Colombia, Brazil, France, Germany, Italy, Turkey, the United Kingdom, and the United States.[^cite_note-474][^cite_note-475]

In the case of the United States, the US Embassy in Beirut announced on 27 September 2024 that it was "not evacuating U.S. citizens at this time."[^cite_note-476][^cite_note-477] In response, US Representative [Rashida Tlaib](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rashida_Tlaib) stated the [State Department](31975.md) was "leaving Americans behind and failing to protect their own citizens".[^cite_note-478] After the US government announced a first flight, [Al Jazeera](30204052.md) wrote: "State Department spokesperson [Matthew Miller](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Miller_\(spokesperson\)) said the flight carried 100 US citizens – a fraction of the nearly 6,000 Americans who have contacted the US embassy for information and help."[^cite_note-479] [Lebanese-Americans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lebanese_Americans) filed a class-action lawsuit against the State Department in the hopes of expediting an evacuation.[^cite_note-480]

Evacuation has been taking place through the use of the countries' military aircraft, chartered flights or maritime routes via [Cyprus](5593.md). As of 5 October 2024, many airlines have indefinitely shut their services at [Beirut–Rafic Hariri International Airport](12869180.md) due to Israeli airstrikes hitting areas nearby, though Lebanon's [Middle East Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_East_Airlines) and regional carriers such as [Iraqi Airways](179397.md) and [Saudi Arabian Airlines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saudi_Arabian_Airlines) have been operating.[^cite_note-481]

## Analysis

### United States' waning influence

Writing for *[The Guardian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Guardian)*, Andrew Roth assessed that the invasion showed Israel's willingness to ignore its chief ally, the United States, and the latter's lack of influence over the Netanyahu government. [Aaron David Miller](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aaron_David_Miller), a senior fellow at the [Carnegie Endowment for International Peace](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carnegie_Endowment_for_International_Peace), stated that this was in part caused by the [Democrats](5043544.md) trying to avoid criticizing Netanyahu before the [US presidential elections](59568546.md). Analysts believe that Netanyahu had a limited time frame before the US elections to attack Iranian proxies in the region.[^cite_note-p448-482] Writers for [ITV News](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ITV_News), *[The Washington Post](102226.md)*, and *[Politico](9172097.md)* agreed that the invasion highlighted the waning influence the US government has on Israel.[^cite_note-p566-483][^cite_note-c158-484][^cite_note-h944-485]

### Personal motivations of Netanyahu

According to [CNN](62028.md)'s Stephen Collinson as well as Israeli observers, there is a long-standing suspicion among observers in Washington that Netanyahu has a strong personal interest in continuing the war to make amends for his failure to prevent the [7 October attacks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Hamas-led_attack_on_Israel) and to delay ongoing [legal proceedings against him](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trial_of_Benjamin_Netanyahu) for bribery, fraud, and breach of public trust.[^cite_note-486][^cite_note-o271-487]

### Hezbollah capabilities

Prior the ground invasion, the [Institute for the Study of War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Institute_for_the_Study_of_War) (ISW) on September 28 noted that while [Israeli strikes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/September_2024_Lebanon_strikes) led to "temporary organizational paralysis" and "internal disarray", Hezbollah "should be able to overcome this disruption, however, if given the time and space to do so", and furthermore that killed Hezbollah commanders "have deputies who should in principle be able to fill those roles and help the force recover" despite "difficult circumstance". The ISW termed damage done to Hezbollah's leadership as "short term" and a "temporary effect" if given the chance to reconstitute.[^cite_note-iran-update-September-28-2024-39]

On 5 October, the ISW reported that Hezbollah capabilities may have not been as degraded as previously thought, despite the extensive Israeli killing of its commanders and likely reduction in combat effectiveness of some of its forces. The report stated that Hezbollah has kept the bulk of its forces in positions deeper inside Lebanon while only engaging in limited-contact attacks against the IDF at the frontlines, concluding that the group "retains effective command-and-control at least at the tactical level and possibly higher".[^cite_note-488] Writing for the [Center for Strategic and International Studies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Center_for_Strategic_and_International_Studies), Daniel Byman assessed that Israel had learnt lessons from the [2006 Lebanon War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_Lebanon_War) and had a number of intelligence successes prior to the invasion, such as [the detonation of pagers used by Hezbollah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Lebanon_pager_explosions). However, Byman also noted that Hezbollah had expanded its rocket arsenal since, and benefited from a number of factors such as being able to retreat away from the border and dragging the war out to its benefit.[^cite_note-i3642-489] The *[New Statesman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Statesman)* noted that though Israel enjoyed military superiority over Hezbollah, the latter had been able to bounce back from prior military defeats, and could exploit the fact that Israel is unlikely to win the conflict on the battlefield alone.[^cite_note-r415-490]

The ISW reported on 28 October that Hezbollah's military forces in southern Lebanon were disorganized and struggling against the IDF, which have effectively disrupted their operations. It also claimed that despite Hezbollah's attempts to portray itself as a competent military organization, it had failed to execute significant military strategies and lost valuable equipment to the IDF.[^cite_note-491]

### Israel's methods

See also: [Dahiya doctrine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dahiya_doctrine)

According to Karim Emilie Bitar, a professor of international relations at the [Saint Joseph University of Beirut](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saint_Joseph_University_of_Beirut), IDF airstrikes outside of south Lebanon are meant to foster civil strife.[^cite_note-492]

The incident this morning (Wednesday), in which six Nut fighters fell , began around 04\:30, with an attack launched by Egoz fighters who encountered terrorists in the building. Immediately after the encounter, heavy fire began on the forces from small arms, anti-tank weapons and rocket launchers. The forces began a major evacuation effort of the casualties under heavy fire and the bravery shown by the medical and rescue forces from the ground and the air of Unit 669. Even before the incident, yesterday there were two face-to-face encounters that ended with the elimination of six terrorists by the Egoz fighters without any soldiers being injured, and in total about 50 Hezbollah terrorists were eliminated by the commando fighters since the beginning of the operation on Monday night.

Even before Mr. Nasrallah decided to expand pager usage, Israel had put into motion a plan to establish a shell company that would pose as an international pager producer. By all appearances, B.A.C. Consulting was a Hungary-based company that was under contract to produce the devices on behalf of a Taiwanese company, Gold Apollo. In fact, it was part of an Israeli front, according to three intelligence officers briefed on the operation. They said at least two other shell companies were created as well to mask the real identities of the people creating the pagers: Israeli intelligence officers.

[^cite_note-160]: &#x20;Hagari, Daniel \[@IDFspokesperson] (30 September 2024). ["בהתאם להחלטת הדרג המדיני, צה״ל החל לפני מספר שעות בפעולה קרקעית ממוקדת ומתוחמת במרחב דרום לבנון נגד יעדי ותשתיות טרור של ארגון הטרור חיזבאללה, במספר כפרים סמוכים לגבול, מהם נשקף איום מיידי וממשי ליישובים ישראלים בגבול הצפון. צה״ל פועל על פי תוכנית סדורה שתוכננה במטה הכללי ובפיקוד הצפון, ואליה הכוחות התאמנו ונערכו בחודשים האחרונים. הכוחות הקרקעיים מלווים במאמץ תקיפות של חיל האוויר וכוחות תותחנים, שתוקפים מטרות צבאיות באיזור במאמץ מתואם עם לוחמי כוחות היבשה. שלבי המערכה אושרו ומתבצעים בהתאם להחלטת הדרג המדיני. מבצע ״חיצי הצפון״ נמשך על פי הערכת המצב במקביל ללחימה בעזה ובזירות נוספות. צה״ל ממשיך להילחם ולפעול להשגת מטרות המלחמה, ועושה כל מה שנדרש על מנת להגן על אזרחי מדינת ישראל ולהשיב את תושבי הצפון לבתיהם בביטחון. אנו מבקשים לא להעביר שמועות ודיווחים לא רשמיים על אודות פעילות כוחות צה״ל, ולהיצמד להודעות הרשמיות בלבד"](https://x.com/IDFspokesperson/status/1840889177023361325) \[In accordance with the decision of the political echelon, the IDF began a few hours ago a targeted and delimited ground operation in southern Lebanon against terrorist targets and infrastructures of the terrorist organization Hezbollah, in a number of villages near the border, which pose an immediate and real threat to Israeli settlements on the northern border. The IDF operates according to a regular plan designed by the General Headquarters and the Northern Command, for which the forces have trained and prepared in recent months. The ground forces are accompanied in an attack effort by the air force and artillery forces, which attack military targets in the area in a coordinated effort with the fighters of the ground forces. The stages of the campaign have been approved and are being carried out in accordance with the decision of the political level. Operation "Northern Arrows" continues according to the assessment of the situation at the same time as the fighting in Gaza and other arenas. The IDF continues to fight and act to achieve the goals of the war, and does everything necessary to protect the citizens of the State of Israel and return the residents of the north to their homes safely. We ask not to pass on rumors and unofficial reports about the activities of the IDF forces, and to stick to the official announcements only] ([Tweet](68533868.md)) (in Hebrew). Retrieved 30 September 2024 – via [X (formerly Twitter)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X_\(formerly_Twitter\)).

[^cite_note-Franceinfo-456]: &#x20;["Guerre au Proche-Orient : le ministre des Affaires étrangères, Jean-Noël Barrot, dénonce une "provocation" de Benyamin Nétanyahou, qui menace le Liban de "destructions" comme à Gaza"](https://www.francetvinfo.fr/monde/proche-orient/guerre/guerre-au-proche-orient-le-ministre-des-affaires-etrangeres-jean-noel-barrot-denonce-une-provocation-de-benyamin-netanyahou-qui-menace-le-liban-de-destructions-comme-a-gaza_6826154.html) (in French). francetvinfo.fr. 8 October 2024. Retrieved 8 October 2024. Le ministre français des Affaires étrangères, Jean-Noël Barrot a dénoncé, mardi 8 octobre sur France 2, une "provocation" du Premier ministre israélien Benyamin Nétanyahou, qui a menacé le Liban de "destructions et (de) souffrances comme celles que nous voyons à Gaza" s'il ne se débarrassait pas du Hezbollah.

Retrieved 8 October 2024. "Le Liban est un pays ami de la France, déjà si fragilisé, entraîné dans une guerre qu'il n'a pas choisi", a déclaré Jean-Noël Barrot. "La France se tient aux côtés du Liban dans les moments les plus durs", a ajouté le ministre.
